### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para actualizar tu rama local o privada con la rama base y limpiar tus commits antes de compartirlos. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es pública o compartida, porque reescribir su historial le causaría problemas a quien ya la tiene.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠传输：先建立连接，保证数据按序送达、丢了会重传；UDP 不建连接也不保证送达，但开销小、延迟低。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch to tidy its commits before review. Merge once the branch is shared or public, because rebasing rewrites history that others may already have pulled.
```

bun test test/foo.test.ts

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to data on disk before the change is applied to the database itself. If the system crashes mid-write, it replays that log on restart to recover committed changes and roll back incomplete ones.
```


### board-offer-go-en

```
(earlier) user: Run hawdb's write benchmark every night and tell me how it went.
(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.
(earlier) partner: Night 3: 182k writes/s at p99 4.1 ms, level with last night; no compaction stalls.
(earlier) Ducter offered on its own card: keep task "bench"'s rounds on a board?
--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the nightly benchmark board is set up, showing Night 2 and Night 3 so far, and it will fill in each night.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2261 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub">Writes/s and p99 latency from the nightly hawdb write benchmark</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="runs"><div class="k-empty">Each night's run lands here.</div></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  function el(tag, cls, text) {
    var n = document.createElement(tag);
    if (cls) n.className = cls;
    if (text != null) n.textContent = String(text);
    return n;
  }
  function render(v) {
    var runs = Array.isArray(v && v.runs) ? v.runs : (Array.isArray(v) ? v : []);
    var latest = document.getElementById('latest');
    var sub = document.getElementById('latest-sub');
    var list = document.getElementById('runs');
    list.textContent = '';
    if (!runs.length) {
      latest.textContent = 'No runs yet';
      latest.classList.add('k-none');
      list.appendChild(el('div', 'k-empty', "Each night's run lands here."));
      if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) ducter.summary('No runs yet');
      return;
    }
    latest.classList.remove('k-none');
    var last = runs[runs.length - 1];
    latest.textContent = (last.writes || '—') + ' writes/s';
    sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + (last.p99 || '—') + ' · compaction stalls ' + (last.stalls != null ? last.stalls : '—');
    runs.slice().reverse().forEach(function (r) {
      var row = el('div', 'k-row');
      var main = el('div', 'k-row-main');
      main.appendChild(el('div', '', r.night || 'Night'));
      main.appendChild(el('div', 'k-row-sub', 'p99 ' + (r.p99 || '—') + ' · stalls ' + (r.stalls != null ? r.stalls : '—')));
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(el('div', 'k-row-value', (r.writes || '—') + '/s'));
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) ducter.summary(last.writes + ' writes/s, p99 ' + last.p99);
  }
  if (window.ducter) {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('runs')).then(function (r) { render({ runs: r }); });
    ducter.onChange(function () {
      Promise.resolve(ducter.get('runs')).then(function (r) { render({ runs: r }); });
    });
  }
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":0},{"night":"Night 3","writes":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}
```

```
function dn(s){ var q = s.split('-').map(Number); return Math.round(Date.UTC(q[0], q[1]-1, q[2]) / 864e5); }

// current streak: counts back from today, or from yesterday if today isn't ticked yet
function cur(days){

  var n = 0;
  while (set.has(key(d))) { n++; d.setDate(d.getDate()-1); }
  return n;
}

// best streak ever
function bestRun(days){
  var ns = Array.from(new Set(days)).map(dn).sort(function(a,b){ return a-b; });
  var best = 0, run = 0, prev = null;
  for (var i = 0; i < ns. … (h.days) + '</div>' +

      '</div>';
  }).join('');

}

function save(){

}

document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click', function(e){
  var row = e.target.closest('[data-id]');
  if (!row) return;
  var h = H.find(function(x){ return x.id === row.getAttribute('data-id'); });
  if (!h) return;
  var t = key(new Date());
  var i = h.days.indexOf(t);
  if (i >= 0) h.days.splice(i, 1); else h.days.push(t);
  save();
});
document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
  if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') { e.preventDefault(); e.target.click(); }
});

function load(v){
  if (Array.isArray(v) && v.length) H = v;
  render();
}
render();
if (window.ducter) {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('habits')).then(load);
  if (ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(function(){ Promise.resolve(ducter.get('habits')).then(load); });
}
</script>
```

```
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="left">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub">tick each one off as it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</main>
<style>
  body { margin: 0; background: var(--d-bg); color: var(--d-ink); font-family: -apple-system, system-ui, sans-serif; }
  .k-app { max-width: 360px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 16px; box-sizing: border-box; }
  .k-sum { background: var(--d-card); border: 1px solid var(--d-line); border-radius: 12px; padding: 14px 16px; margin-bottom: 16px; }
  .k-sum-label { font-size: 13px; color: var(--d-muted); }
  .k-sum-value { font-size: 32px; font-weight: 600; color: var(--d-ink); }
  .k-sum-sub { font-size: 13px; color: var(--d-soft); }
  .k-h { font-size: 15px; margin: 0 0 8px; color: var(--d-ink); }
  .k-list { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; }
  .k-row { display: flex; align-items: flex-start; gap: 12px; background: var(--d-card); border: 1px solid var(--d-line); border-radius: 10px; padding: 12px; cursor: pointer; text-align: left; width: 100%; font: inherit; color: inherit; }
  .k-b … 13px; color: var(--d-soft); margin-top: 2px; }
  .k-row-wait { font-size: 13px; color: var(--d-muted); margin-top: 2px; }
</style>
<script>
  var items = ducter.get("items") || [];

  function done() { return ducter.get("done") || {}; }

  function render() {
    var d = done();
    var list = document.getElementById("list");
    list.innerHTML = "";
    var left = 0;
    items.forEach(function (it) {
      var on = !!d[it.id];
      if (!on) left++;
      var row = document.createElement("button");
      row.className = "k-row";
      row.setAttribute("aria-pressed", on ? "true" : "false");
      row.innerHTML =
        '<span class="k-box">' + (on ? "✓" : "") + '</span>' +
        '<span><div class="k-row-main">' + it.title + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">' + it.repo + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-wait">' + it.waiting + '</div></span>';
      row.onclick = function () {
        var cur = done();
        cur[it.id] = !cur[it.id];
        ducter.set("done", cur);
      };
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    document.getElementById("left").textContent = left;
    ducter.summary(left + " still waiting");
  }

  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"Compaction cut","repo":"wey-gu/ducter #412","waiting":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"Retry on 502","repo":"nowledge-co/mem #10287","waiting":"CI failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592","waiting":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"Apps bar","repo":"wey-gu/ducter #418","waiting":"Draft"}]}

```

```

  [partner message] 做好了，已把高铁和酒店两笔填进去，合计 ¥1,698，以后记一笔会自动加总。
  [an app card under the message: changed "出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 3536 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">这趟出差合计</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div><div class="k-empty" id="empty">还没有花销，下面记第一笔。</div></div>
<h2 class="k-h" id="formTitle">记一笔</h2>
<div class="k-card">
<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" placeholder="北京出差 11/20–21"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="11/20"></div>
<div class="k-chips" id="cats"><button type="button" class="k-chip" aria-pressed="true" data-c="交通">交通</button><button type="button" class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="住宿">住宿</button></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
<button class="k-btn" id="del" hidden>删除这笔</button>
</div>
</main>
<script>
let editing=-1;
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
function items(){return ducter.get("items")||[]}
function fmt(n){return "¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("en-US")}
function cat(){const  …    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent=fmt(x.amt||0);
    row.appendChild(main);row.appendChild(val);
    row.onclick=()=>edit(i);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  ducter.summary(xs.length? xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total) : "还没记");
}
function edit(i){const x=items()[i];if(!x)return;editing=i;$("trip").value=x.trip||"";$("day").value=x.day||"";$("amt").value=x.amt||"";setCat(x.cat||"交通");$("save").textContent="保存修改";$("del").hidden=false;$("formTitle").textContent="改这一笔";window.scrollTo(0,document.body.scrollHeight)}
document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>setCat(b.dataset.c));
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=Number($("amt").value);
  if(!$("amt").value||isNaN(amt))return;
  const rec={trip:$("trip").value.trim()||"未命名",day:$("day").value.trim(),cat:cat(),amt:amt};
  const xs=items();
  if(editing>=0&&xs[editing]){rec.note=xs[editing].note||"";xs[editing]=rec}else xs.push(rec);
  ducter.set("items",xs);clearForm();draw();
};
$("del").onclick=()=>{if(editing<0)return;const xs=items();xs.splice(editing,1);ducter.set("items",xs);clearForm();draw()};
ducter.onChange(()=>draw());
clearForm();draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 07:00 上海虹桥到北京南"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```
